bait.setSize("6");\n            bait.setColor(context.getString(R.string.default_color_silver));\n            bait.addPhoto(spinner);\n            Logbook.addBait(bait);\n\n            bait = new Bait("Rippin\' Rap", baitCategory);\n            bait.setType(Bait.TYPE_ARTIFICIAL);\n            bait.setSize("2");\n            bait.setColor(context.getString(R.string.default_color_blue));\n            bait.addPhoto(rap);\n            Logbook.addBait(bait);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void cleanup(Context context) {\n        // delete backup file if one exists\n        String backupFile = LogbookPreferences.getBackupFile(context);\n        if (backupFile != null) {\n            FileUtils.deleteQuietly(new File(backupFile));\n            LogbookPreferences.setBackupFile(context, null);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public static void updatePreferences(Context context) {\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Completely resets the database. This is done in a background thread.\n     */\n    public static void resetAsync(final Context context, final boolean addDefaults,\n                                  final OnResetListener callbacks)\n    {\n        final Handler handler = new Handler();\n\n        Runnable r = new Runnable() {\n            @Override\n            public void run() {\n                reset(context, addDefaults);\n\n                if (callbacks != null)\n                    handler.post(new Runnable() {\n                        @Override\n                        public void run() {\n                            callbacks.onFinish();\n                        }\n                    });\n            }\n        };\n\n        new Thread(r).start();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Completely resets the Logbook\'s data. This cannot be undone.\n     * @param addDefaults True to add default objects after reset; false otherwise.\n     */\n    public static void reset(Context context, boolean addDefaults) {\n        File data = new File(mDatabase.getPath());\n        mDatabase.close();\n        FileUtils.deleteQuietly(data);\n\n        init(context);\n\n        if (addDefaults)\n            setDefaults(context);\n\n        PhotoUtils.cleanPhotos(context);\n    }\n\n    //region Getters & Setters\n    public static SQLiteDatabase getDatabase() {\n        return mDatabase;\n    }\n\n    public static String getName() {\n        return TAG;\n    }\n    //endregion\n\n    //region Backup\n    public static void importFromUri(\n            Context context, Uri uri, Importer.OnProgressListener onProgress)\n    {\n " + CatchTable.Columns.ID + " FROM " + CatchTable.NAME + ")",\n                null\n        );\n\n        numDeleted += mDatabase.delete(\n                BaitPhotoTable.NAME,\n                BaitPhotoTable.Columns.USER_DEFINE_ID + " NOT IN(SELECT " + BaitTable.Columns.ID + " FROM " + BaitTable.NAME + ")",\n                null\n        );\n\n        Log.i(TAG, "Deleted " + numDeleted + " WHERE clause arguments.\n     * @return An ArrayList of {@link Bait} objects matching the WHERE clause.\n     */\n    private static ArrayList<UserDefineObject> getBaits(String whereClause, String[] args) {\n        if (whereClause.isEmpty())\n            whereClause = null;\n\n        return QueryHelper.queryUserDefines(QueryHelper.queryBaits(whereClause, args), new QueryHelper.UserDefineQueryInterface() {\n            @Override\n            public UserDefineObject getObject(UserDefineCursor cursor) {\n                return new BaitCursor(cursor).getBait();\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    public static Bait getBait(UUID id) {\n        return getBait(BaitTable.Columns.ID, id.toString());\n    }\n\n    public static Bait getBait(String name, UUID If null, all\n     *                    possible baits will be returned.\n     * @param sortingMethod A {@link SortingMethod} instance used to sort the {@link Bait} objects\n     *                      for each {@link BaitCategory}.\n     * @return An ArrayList of BaitCategory and Bait objects.\n     */\n    public static ArrayList<UserDefineObject> getBaitsAndCategories(\n            Context context, String searchQuery, SortingMethod sortingMethod)\n    {\n        ArrayList<UserDefineObject> result = new ArrayList<>();\n        ArrayList<UserDefineObject> categories = getBaitCategories();\n\n        for (UserDefineObject category : categories) {\n            result.add(category);\n            result.addAll(UserDefineArrays.searchAndSort(\n                    context, getBaits((BaitCategory) category), searchQuery, sortingMethod));\n        }\n\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    public static ArrayList<Stats.Quantity> getBaitUsedCount() {\n        return getCatchQuantity(getBaits(), new OnQueryQuantityListener() {\n            @Override\n            public int query(UserDefineObject obj) {\n                return QueryHelper.queryUserDefineCatchCount(obj, CatchTable.Columns.BAIT_ID);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets a list of {@link com.cohenadair.anglerslog.model.Stats.Quantity} objects representing\n     * the number of {@link Bait} objects that were used to catch a given {@link Species}.\n     *\n     * @param species The {@link Species} object used to query the database.\n     * @return A list of {@link com.cohenadair.anglerslog.model.Stats.Quantity} objects.\n     */\n    public static ArrayList<Stats.Quantity> getBaitUsedCount(final Species species) {\n        return getCatchQuantity(getBaits(), new OnQueryQuantityListener() {\n            @Override\n            public int query(UserDefineObject obj) {\n                return QueryHelper.queryUserDefineCatchCount(\n                        CatchTable.Columns.BAIT_ID + " = ? args) {\n        if (whereClause.isEmpty())\n            whereClause = null;\n\n        return QueryHelper.queryUserDefines(QueryHelper.queryBaits(whereClause, args), new         }\n        });\n    }\n\n    public static Bait getBait(UUID id) {\n        return getBait(BaitTable.Columns.ID, id.toString());\n    }\n\n    public static Bait getBait(String name, UUID If null, all\n     *                    possible baits will be returned.\n     * @param sortingMethod A {@link SortingMethod} instance used to sort the {@link Bait} objects\n     *                      for each {@link BaitCategory}.\n     * @return An ArrayList of BaitCategory and Bait objects.\n     */\n    public static ArrayList<UserDefineObject> getBaitsAndCategories(\n            Context context, String searchQuery, SortingMethod sortingMethod)\n    {\n        ArrayList<UserDefineObject> result = new ArrayList<>();\n        ArrayList<UserDefineObject> categories = getBaitCategories();\n\n        for (UserDefineObject category : categories) {\n            result.add(category);\n            result.addAll(UserDefineArrays.searchAndSort(\n                    context, getBaits((BaitCategory) category), searchQuery, sortingMethod));\n        }\n\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    public static ArrayList<Stats.Quantity> getBaitUsedCount() {\n        return getCatchQuantity(getBaits(), new OnQueryQuantityListener() {\n            @Override\n            public int query(UserDefineObject obj) {\n                return QueryHelper.queryUserDefineCatchCount(obj, CatchTable.Columns.BAIT_ID);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Gets a list of {@link com.cohenadair.anglerslog.model.Stats.Quantity} objects representing\n     * the number of {@link Bait} objects that were used to catch a given {@link Species}.\n     *\n     * @param species The {@link Species} object used to query the database.\n     * @return A list of {@link com.cohenadair.anglerslog.model.Stats.Quantity} objects.\n     */\n    public static ArrayList<Stats.Quantity> getBaitUsedCount(final Species species) {\n        return getCatchQuantity(getBaits(), new OnQueryQuantityListener() {\n            @Override\n            public int query(UserDefineObject obj) {\n                return QueryHelper.queryUserDefineCatchCount(\n                        CatchTable.Columns.BAIT_ID + " = ? return tripExists(trip, null);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Checks to see if the given {@link Trip} overlaps an existing {@link Trip} in the database.\n     *\n     * @param trip The {@link Trip} to check.\n     * @param excludeId The id of a {@link Trip} to ignore, or null to check against all trips.\n     *                  This is needed when editing an existing {@link Trip}; otherwise the\n     *                  {@link Trip} being edited would always overlap itself and could never have\n     *                  its dates updated.\n     * @return True if the given {@link Trip} overlaps an existing {@link Trip}; false otherwise.\n     */\n    public static boolean tripExists(Trip trip, @Nullable UUID excludeId) {\n        ArrayList<UserDefineObject> trips = getTrips();\n\n        for (UserDefineObject object : trips) {\n            // a trip can\'t overlap itself\n            if (excludeId != null &&\n                    (excludeId.equals(object.getId()) ||\n                            (trip.getId() != null && trip.getId().equals(object.getId()))))\n                continue;\n\n            if (trip.overlapsTrip((Trip)object))\n                return true;\n        }\n\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    public static ArrayList<UserDefineObject> getTrips() {\n        return QueryHelper.queryUserDefines(QueryHelper.queryTrips(null, null), new QueryHelper.UserDefineQueryInterface() {\n            @Override\n            public UserDefineObject getObject(UserDefineCursor cursor) {\n                return new TripCursor(cursor).getTrip();\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    public static ArrayList<UserDefineObject> getTrips(\n            Context context, String searchQuery, SortingMethod sortingMethod)\n    {\n        return UserDefineArrays.searchAndSort(context, getTrips(), searchQuery, sortingMethod);\n    }\n\n    public static ArrayList<UserDefineObject> getTrips(Context context, String searchQuery) {\n        return getTrips(context, searchQuery, null);\n    }\n\n    public static Trip getTrip(UUID id) {\n        UserDefineObject obj = QueryHelper.queryUserDefine(TripTable.NAME, id, new QueryHelper.UserDefineQueryInterface() {\n            @Override\n            public UserDefineObject getObject(UserDefineCursor cursor) {\n                return new TripCursor(cursor).getTrip();\n            }\n        });\n\n        return (obj == null) ? null : (Trip)obj;\n    }\n\n    public static boolean addTrip(Trip trip) {\n        return QueryHelper.insertQuery(TripTable.NAME, trip.getContentValues());\n    }\n\n    public static boolean removeTrip(UUID id) {\n        return removeUserDefine(TripTable.NAME, Logbook.getTrip(id));\n    }\n\n    public static boolean AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='The build succeeds. Here\'s a summary of the diagnosis and fix.\n\n## Root cause\n\nWhen saving an edited trip, `ManageTripFragment.verifyUserInput()` ran an overlap check:\n\n```java\nif ((mDateChanged || !isEditing()) && Logbook.tripExists(getNewTrip())) {\n    AlertUtils.showError(getActivity(), R.string.error_trip_date);\n    return false;\n}\n```\n\n`mDateChanged` is set to `true` (via `mDateChanged = isEditing()`) as soon as the user picks a new start/end date while editing, so the check runs on every date edit.\n\n`Logbook.tripExists()` loops over **every** trip in the database and returns `true` if the candidate overlaps any of them — including the very trip being edited. Since the edited object is a copy that keeps the original id (`new Trip((Trip) oldObject, true)`) and its new date range necessarily overlaps its own stored range in the common case (e.g. moving an end date later, or nudging either date within/over the existing span), `tripExists()` always returned `true`. The save was therefore aborted with the "trip dates overlap" error, making it impossible to edit a trip\'s dates at all.\n\n## Fix\n\n1. **`Logbook.java`** — added an overload `tripExists(Trip trip, @Nullable UUID excludeId)` that skips the stored trip matching `excludeId` (and the candidate\'s own id) while still checking against every other trip. The original single-argument `tripExists(Trip)` now delegates with `null`, so existing callers/tests keep their previous semantics.\n\n2. **`ManageTripFragment.java`** — the save-time validation now passes the id of the trip under edit (`isEditing() ? getEditingId() : null`), so a trip is no longer compared against itself. Using `getEditingId()` rather than just the new object\'s id also covers the edge case where the old object could not be loaded and a blank object with a fresh id was created.\n\nGenuine overlaps with *other* trips are still rejected, and the add-a-new-trip path is unchanged.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 932, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 51932, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 932, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfYLULzETvS5G3s7HgbMo', stop_reason=None, session_id='4fe8d8d8-56b6-42d7-a4a7-670448687bd5', uuid='9d3f1864-6b6b-495d-8a17-c6be76173e4d')
